# Dojo World — Persistent State

**Purpose:** the single living state document for Dojo World, maintained by Muse so ChatGPT can evaluate the project's current state at any time. This file is overwritten in place on every refresh — the copy in the Drive relay folder is canonical for review. A relay/state document never authorizes work by itself; Kev's direct word in chat governs.

**Maintained by:** Muse (orchestrator/verifier). Developer: Muse (takeover from 2026-10-30 ~17:15 CT after Claude API credit exhaustion; Claude was developer via narrow, pre-measured dispatches before that). Creative direction and all verdicts: Kev.
**Last refreshed:** 2026-10-31 ~16:45 CT (PROMOTED no-163 merge per Kev's 2026-10-31 ~16:19 authorization of 159–166 with 163 held — see new current entry below; previous live e9112cf7 backed up to hidden_files/baseline-backup-20261019/vf-default-pre-promote-159-162-165-166-20261031.html). Previous: 2026-10-31 ~08:07 CT (REVERTED cycle 158 per Kev's 2026-10-31 ~07:29 throw restart — old-direction Phase C merge rolled back; live build restored to e9112cf7, cycle 155).

## 3. Accepted baseline (locked — do not regress)

- **Art form (Kev, Oct 21):** clean stick figures — uniform limbs, round heads, solid silhouette colors — in the cinematic Fuji scene (golden-hour sky, cherry blossoms, wet reflective ground). The R3 volumetric/gloss rendering is superseded as a render target; the rig/skeleton/weight work underneath stays.
- **DQ25 — BLUE wide low stance ACCEPTED / DEFAULT** (Kev, Oct 27 ~01:18: "Ok yea that stance is perfect."; promoted cycle 56, build 648ac89d): pelvis 0.70, feet 1.79 m apart, both knees bent (front 134.9° / rear 120.7°), balanced grounded COG, lead arm slight bend 142.4°, rear arm high and behind, hand-to-hand diagonal 1.478 m. This is BLUE's style stance (directed exception to the shared straight stance); DQ22/DQ23 stance geometry is superseded (history in the run log).
- **DQ8 — near-vertical stance ACCEPTED** (Kev, Oct 22: "Ok much better"): idle/guard knees ~167–168°; legs almost completely vertical in the shared neutral stance.
- **Natural weight rules:** stance generally straight; EACH LEG SUPPORTS WEIGHT INDEPENDENTLY — one planted support leg carries the load under the COM, the other is free; never mirrored legs. Acceptance is measured per leg.
- **DQ9–DQ12 — blue drop-stance family:** front leg long/straight toward the opponent, trailing leg deep crouch with knee flared AWAY, rear arm up over the head; pelvis ~0.82.
- **DQ3 — depth ordering fixed** (35 violations → 0).
- **DQ13 — blue torso vertical in the lunge** (lean 30.2° → 14.3° off vertical). ACCEPTED by Kev Oct 24 23:54 ("The progress looks good. I'm impressed actually.").
- **DQ14a — punch realism** (shoulder rotation 0.22 rad, hips 0.08, rear-hand pullback, non-mirrored knees 169.0°/165.1°). ACCEPTED Oct 24 23:54.
- **DQ14b — kick/launcher realism** (pelvis over support foot, XZ offset 0.128 → 0.072 m; hip −0.048 vs shoulder +0.176 counter-rotation; rear-arm counter-swing). ACCEPTED Oct 24 23:54.
- **Throw vs sidestep stays POSITIONAL** (Kev ruling, Oct 20): a sidestep dodges a throw only by physically leaving grab range — never state-based. Do not re-raise.
- **Long-term vision (Kev, Oct 20):** Dojo World becomes a persistent open-world MMO mobile game; the 1v1 fighter is the combat foundation. A unified movement/balance system is a FUTURE milestone — it does not authorize scope expansion or a physics overhaul now.
- **World design note (Kev, Oct 25 — verbatim at `files/dojo-world-design-note-20261025.md`):** Fight cam — in-world TVs showing the highest-profile fights, with rankings and game news during downtime. Stages should feel **lived in and gritty** (general stage direction; the prototype's Fuji stage stays per the canonical reference unless Kev directs a rework). World features: training for tournaments, tournaments, story mode/side missions, shopping for cosmetics/customization, social interactions, random world events.

## 5. Governance (adopted Oct 25, 00:26 CT)

- ChatGPT's Development Priority Directive governs priorities (DQ15 → DQ17 → DQ16; DQ10/DQ6/DQ7/DQ1/DQ4 alongside) **with Kev's amendment: GPT oversight is advisory via the relay and never gates a cycle. Kev alone verifies results and closes design questions, in chat, as before — the loop does not wait for GPT.**
- Priority + deliverable directive (Kev direct, Oct 25 05:24 CT): DQ17 weighted falls FIRST, then DQ16 impact scaling, then additional striking polish. Preserve the accepted proportions, stances, DQ15 walking, Fuji environment, and combat functionality. One measurable animation behavior per cycle. Nightly recordings must include the featured movement at NORMAL SPEED and in SLOW MOTION, full arc from anticipation through recovery. No ragdoll, no wholesale animation-engine replacement.
- GPT review loop (Kev direct, Oct 25 04:57 CT): instructions issued via relay file `claude-to-chatgpt-dojo-world-state-review-instructions-2026-10-25.md` — GPT reviews this state document daily after the nightly refresh (report video whenever the state changed, plus a dated relay note every review), runs a deep evaluation every Monday, and creates its own scheduled task for that cadence, confirming the schedule back via relay. Awaiting GPT's confirmation note.
- Confidentiality hold: internal only. No publishing, filing, outreach, or spending beyond the approved Claude API use without Kev's explicit approval. Portfolio program excepted as Kev approved (résumés + case study delivered; GitHub/demo are GPT's side).
- Copyright: protect at least one mark (recommendation: Dojo World first) before full development. $0 spent, nothing filed.

## 7. Full current source — prototype/index.html

Build sha256: `786ac6bf36413459be491609ebe986106de0a09b4753e233854cad80f0e2968b`

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Dojo World — mobile fighting prototype</title>
<style>
:root{--bg:#eceae4;--fg:#1b1b1f;--panel:#fff;--accent:#d6452b;--blue:#2b6fd6;--ring:#d6d0c0;box-sizing:border-box;padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){--bg:#0f1115;--fg:#eee;--panel:#1d2128;--accent:#ff6a4d;--blue:#5a9bff;--ring:#272c35}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--bg:#0f1115;--fg:#eee;--panel:#1d2128;--accent:#ff6a4d;--blue:#5a9bff;--ring:#272c35}
html,body{height:100%;margin:0}
body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;overflow:hidden;touch-action:none;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none}
#app{height:100%;display:flex;flex-direction:column}
#c{flex:1;min-height:0;width:100%;display:block}
#pad{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:8px 14px 12px;gap:10px}
#dp{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,54px);grid-auto-rows:46px;gap:5px}
#ab{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,64px);grid-auto-rows:46px;gap:5px}
button{background:var(--panel);color:var(--fg);border:1px solid var(--ring);border-radius:12px;font:600 16px system-ui;touch-action:none}
button.on{background:var(--accent);color:#fff}
#u{grid-column:2}
</style></head><body>
<div id="app"><canvas id="c"></canvas>
<div id="pad">
<div id="dp"><button id="u" data-k="u">▲</button><button data-k="l" style="grid-row:2">◀</button><button data-k="d" style="grid-row:2">▼</button><button data-k="r" style="grid-row:2">▶</button></div>
<div id="ab"><button data-k="p">P</button><button data-k="k">K</button><button data-k="g">G</button><button data-k="pk">P+K</button></div>
</div></div>
<script>
// ================= CONFIG =================
const DT=1/60, GRAV=24, RING=4, FWD=2.3, BACK=1.5, STEPV=3.4;
const CHAIN_DRIFT=0.030, CHAIN_CROUCH=0.22, CHAIN_LIFT=0.13; // C1/C2: chained-link forward drift per frame (startup+active only), crouch depth, step lift.
const CYCLE_DIST=1.12, STEP_LEN=.56;   // R2: metres per full gait cycle; stance sweep length (= CYCLE_DIST/2, so stance speed == body speed)
// Moves: su=startup, ac=active, rc=recovery (frames); reach/h in meters; kb=knockback
const M={
  P:{su:6, ac:3,rc:10,dmg:8, reach:.85,h:1.35,kb:2.2,hs:14,bs:7},
  K:{su:9, ac:4,rc:18,dmg:14,reach:1.15,h:1.0, kb:3.2,hs:20,bs:12,lunge:1.5},  // KickSpeed (Kev 2026-10-26): kick strike phase a little faster - su 11->9 only; ac/rc/reach/dmg unchanged
  L:{su:13,ac:3,rc:28,dmg:16,reach:1.0, h:.9,  kb:4.0,hs:30,bs:18,launch:7},  // KickSpeed: launcher kick su 16->13 only; ac/rc/reach/dmg/launch unchanged
  T:{su:5, ac:2,rc:26,dmg:22,reach:.75,h:1.1, kb:3,  hs:30,bs:0, throw:1}
};
// DQ107 PHASE-SYSTEM REFACTOR (Kev 2026-10-29: refactor the two DQ105 throws into the pasted phase system).
// STRUCTURAL ONLY: every number below is the literal DQ103/DQ104/DQ105 number moved into the table;
// the throw code below reads the table instead of literals, in the same arithmetic order, so traces are identical.
// Phase shape (pasted design, mapped to this 2D side view): phases[] with timed windows + per-phase
// thrower/victim keyframe targets, and a launch event. NO new throws: the pasted suplex/shoulderThrow/
// heavyChuck are NOT added. Current M.T numbers preserved (reach .75, damage 22, DQ99 arc as the flight
// phase, DQ104 carry as the carry mechanism re-expressed as phases). NOT in scope: handSocket/attachTo
// (carry anchoring stays), hitstop/cameraShake (not added); 3D launch vectors -> facing-axis + height only.
function throwRamp(t,ph){const u0=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(t-ph.up[0])/(ph.up[1]-ph.up[0]))),u=u0*u0*(3-2*u0),d0=t<=ph.hold?1:Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(ph.down-t)/(ph.down-ph.hold))),d=d0*d0*(3-2*d0);return u*d}
function throwPhase(th,name){for(const p of th.phases)if(p.name===name)return p;return null}
const THROW_PHASES={
 lunge:{name:'blueArmGrabTurn',phases:[
  {name:'grab',up:[5,11],hold:16,down:23,thrower:{hl:[0,.35,.10],hr:[0,0,0],pel:[0,0,0]}},
  {name:'carry',lift:.60,liftFrames:10,release:12,victim:{turn:.85,holdArm:[-.15,.85,-.38]}},
  {name:'launch',vy:4.8,vx:3,arc:'DQ99'}]},
 back:{name:'redClinchHipToss',phases:[
  {name:'grab',up:[5,11],hold:16,down:23,thrower:{hl:[.05,.28,-.17],hr:[.05,.28,.17],pel:[.06,-.04,0]}},
  {name:'carry',lift:.60,liftFrames:10,release:12,victim:{pitch:.35}},
  {name:'launch',vy:4.8,vx:3,arc:'DQ99'}]},
 // DQ108 SHOULDER THROW (Kev 2026-10-29: new phase-table entry from the filed shoulder-sequence reference; PROPOSED reach 0.70 / damage 14, NOT approved values; harness-demonstrable only this cycle — style binding open, no live binding, no tryHit/M.T/AI/input change).
 shoulder:{name:'shoulderThrow',phases:[
  {name:'grab',up:[5,11],hold:16,down:29,thrower:{hl:[-.05,-.12,.02],hr:[0,-.15,-.02],pel:[.08,-.10,0],colR:[-.70,.52,.02],slvL:[-.18,.31,.04]}},
  {name:'carry',lift:.60,liftFrames:10,release:16,carryFrames:16,victim:{overBack:1}},
  {name:'launch',vy:4.8,vx:3,arc:'DQ99',ic:'shoulder'}]},
 ippon:{name:'ipponSeoiNage',phases:[
  {name:'grab',up:[4,14],hold:24,down:38,thrower:{hl:[-.05,-.12,.02],hr:[0,-.15,-.02],pel:[.14,-.16,0],trapA:[-.60,.45,.10],trapN:[-.30,.40,.05]}},
  {name:'carry',lift:.65,liftFrames:18,release:24,carryFrames:24,victim:{overBack:1}},
  {name:'launch',vy:6.0,vx:4.8,arc:'DQ99',ic:'ippon'}]},
};
// Hit test: strike point (reach along facing) must land inside the defender's body radius (~.3m), not merely near its center. Throws are a two-hand grab, so slightly more forgiving.
const HURT=.3, HURT_T=.4;
const cv=document.getElementById('c'),g=cv.getContext('2d');
const css=n=>getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(n).trim();
let W=300,H=300;
function rs(){const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect(),d=devicePixelRatio||1;W=r.width;H=r.height;cv.width=W*d;cv.height=H*d;g.setTransform(d,0,0,d,0,0)}
new ResizeObserver(rs).observe(cv);

// ================= STATE =================
const R=Math.random;
function mk(x,ai){return{x,z:0,y:0,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,hp:100,st:'idle',t:0,dur:0,mv:null,hit:0,ic:'mid',rm:0,gy:0,ax:1,az:0,fx:1,fz:0,sd:0,ai,aiT:0,wins:0,combo:0,cd:0,w:0,imp:1,pc:0,pn:0,kc:0,kn:0,buf:null,kp:14,sdur:0,ssteps:0,samp:0,ssev:0,sdesp:0,sdone:0,srem:0,desp:0,originPose:null,rootX0:0,rootZ0:0,prevTip:null,style:ai?'lunge':'back',chainN:0,chainBlend:0,chainStart:null,i:{l:0,r:0,u:0,d:0,g:0,bp:0,bk:0},legX:{pdL:999,pdR:999,laL:0,laR:0,raL:0,raR:0}}} // LEGFIX: Blue crossover-straightening state per leg (prev foot gap, latch, ramp) — pose() also creates it lazily
let S=[mk(-1.3,0),mk(1.3,1)],phase='intro',pt=0,tm=45*60,round=1,hs=0,shake=0,msg='ROUND 1',msgT=90,cmb=0,cmbT=0,sparks=[];
const cam={yaw:0,mx:0,mz:0,D:11};
const H0={l:0,r:0,u:0,d:0,g:0};

function reset(full){
  const w=[S[0].wins,S[1].wins];
  S=[mk(-1.3,0),mk(1.3,1)];
  if(!full){S[0].wins=w[0];S[1].wins=w[1]}else round=1;
  phase='intro';pt=0;tm=45*60;hs=0;msg='ROUND '+round;msgT=90;cmb=0;sparks=[];
}

// ================= COMBAT =================
// XIAO: universal captured-pose entry (README rule 1) — every attack starts from the pose the fighter is in.
// SUPPORT-FOOT GATE (Xiao canStartMove, Kev 2026-10-27: include support foot gating) — neutral starts only (idle/walk/guard/step); chains (atk) keep DQ18d timing and the stumble desperation strike keeps its own rule, so neither passes through here. A refusal changes nothing (no state, counter, or input touched) so the caller retries when the foot plants. Kicks: K0 needs the rear foot planted, K1/K2 the lead foot; hands/launcher/throw need either foot. Threshold 0.06 (not 0.01): Red's idle lead foot rests at 0.050, so 0.06 counts stance feet as support while clearly airborne swing feet (0.10+) still refuse.
// C2: chain bookkeeping (go from atk = chain cancel).
function go(f,k){if(['idle','walk','guard','step'].includes(f.st)){const q=pose(f),lead=q.fl[0]>=q.fr[0]?'fl':'fr',rear=lead==='fl'?'fr':'fl',planted=j=>f.y+q[j][1]<=0.06;let sup='either';if(k==='K')sup=(f.kc%3)===0?rear:lead;if(!(f.y<=0.01&&(sup==='either'?(planted('fl')||planted('fr')):planted(sup))))return false}f.originPose=pose(f);if(f.st==='atk'){f.chainStart=pose(f);f.chainN=(f.chainN||0)+1;f.chainBlend=1}else{f.chainN=0;f.chainBlend=0};f.rootX0=f.x;f.rootZ0=f.z;f.prevTip=null;f.st='atk';f.mv=M[k];f.t=0;f.hit=0;f.ax=f.fx;f.az=f.fz;f.i.bp=f.i.bk=0;f.buf=null;
  if(k==='P'){f.pn=f.pc%5;f.pc++}   // DQ18b/DQ18c/DQ18e: punch counter cycles the style strike set - RED boxing (jab/cross/hook/uppercut/overhand), BLUE kung fu (lead palm/rear straight/chop/flowing rear palm/sweeping back palm)
  if(k==='K'){f.kn=f.kc%3;f.kc++}   // DQ18c2/DQ18e: kick counter cycles each style's leg set - BLUE kung fu (snapping front kick/sweep/side kick), RED boxing (front kick/roundhouse/crescent kick)
  if(f.mv.lunge){f.vx=f.ax*f.mv.lunge;f.vz=f.az*f.mv.lunge}
  return true;}

function spark(x,y,z,c){sparks.push({x,y,z,t:0,c})}

let XIAO_SWEEP=1; // XIAO SWEPT CONTACT LIVE (Kev 2026-10-27 ~19:49: move forward with Xiao features where logical — swept contact matches hits to the limb's actual sweep; revert by setting 0). // XIAO: swept-tip contact trial (README) — 0 = original fixed-point tryHit (live); 1 = pose-derived swept-tip contact (trial: palm range extends, see probe).
function segSegHit(A,B,o,rad){const sub=(a,b)=>a.map((n,i)=>n-b[i]),add=(a,b)=>a.map((n,i)=>n+b[i]),mul=(a,k)=>a.map(n=>n*k),dot=(a,b)=>a.reduce((s,n,i)=>s+n*b[i],0),cl=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));const d1=sub(B,A),d2=[0,1.8,0],r=sub(A,[o.x,o.y,o.z]),a=dot(d1,d1),e=dot(d2,d2),ff=dot(d2,r);let s=0,t=0;if(a<=1e-9&&e<=1e-9)return dot(r,r)<=rad*rad;if(a<=1e-9)t=cl(ff/e,0,1);else{const c=dot(d1,r);if(e<=1e-9)s=cl(-c/a,0,1);else{const b=dot(d1,d2),dn=a*e-b*b;if(dn>1e-9)s=cl((b*ff-c*e)/dn,0,1);t=(b*s+ff)/e;if(t<0){t=0;s=cl(-c/a,0,1)}else if(t>1){t=1;s=cl((b-c)/a,0,1)}}}const dl=sub(add(A,mul(d1,s)),add([o.x,o.y,o.z],mul(d2,t)));return dot(dl,dl)<=rad*rad;}
function sweepHit(f,o){ // XIAO: pose-derived swept-tip contact (trial only) — tip segment prev->cur frame vs defender vertical capsule; updates f.prevTip; guard/damage/reactions stay in tryHit. DQ87: kicks also test the striking shin segment (knee->foot) vs the capsule, so a kick whose foot has already overshot past a close defender still connects through the shin (shin test uses body radius HURT only, no limb radius, so a sidestepped defender stays evaded); punches keep the tip-only test, and the far-range bound is unchanged (at far range the foot is the closest point on the leg). DQ88: punches also test the striking forearm segment (elbow->fist) vs the capsule at HURT+0.065 (body radius plus the arm's own half-width from ARMW), so a punch whose fist has overshot past a close defender still connects through the forearm; far-range and z=0.8 sidestep evasion unchanged.
  const m=f.mv,limb=m===M.P?((f.pn===1||f.pn===3||f.pn===4)?'hr':'hl'):(f.kn===0&&f.style==='lunge'?'fl':'fr');
  const q=pose(f),L=q[limb],cur=[f.x+f.fx*L[0]-f.fz*L[2],f.y+L[1],f.z+f.fz*L[0]+f.fx*L[2]];
  const prev=f.prevTip||cur,rad=(m===M.P?0.11:0.14)+HURT;
  f.prevTip=cur;
  if(m===M.K){const K=q[limb==='fl'?'kl':'kr'],kw=[f.x+f.fx*K[0]-f.fz*K[2],f.y+K[1],f.z+f.fz*K[0]+f.fx*K[2]];if(segSegHit(kw,cur,o,HURT))return true;}
  if(m===M.P){const E=q[limb==='hl'?'el':'er'],ew=[f.x+f.fx*E[0]-f.fz*E[2],f.y+E[1],f.z+f.fz*E[0]+f.fx*E[2]];if(segSegHit(ew,cur,o,HURT+0.065))return true;}
  const sub=(a,b)=>a.map((n,i)=>n-b[i]),add=(a,b)=>a.map((n,i)=>n+b[i]),mul=(a,k)=>a.map(n=>n*k),dot=(a,b)=>a.reduce((s,n,i)=>s+n*b[i],0),cl=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const d1=sub(cur,prev),d2=[0,1.8,0],r=sub(prev,[o.x,o.y,o.z]),a=dot(d1,d1),e=dot(d2,d2),ff=dot(d2,r);let s=0,t=0;
  if(a<=1e-9&&e<=1e-9) return dot(r,r)<=rad*rad;
  if(a<=1e-9) t=cl(ff/e,0,1);
  else{const c=dot(d1,r);if(e<=1e-9)s=cl(-c/a,0,1);else{const b=dot(d1,d2),dn=a*e-b*b;if(dn>1e-9)s=cl((b*ff-c*e)/dn,0,1);t=(b*s+ff)/e;if(t<0){t=0;s=cl(-c/a,0,1)}else if(t>1){t=1;s=cl((b-c)/a,0,1)}}}
  const dl=sub(add(prev,mul(d1,s)),add([o.x,o.y,o.z],mul(d2,t)));return dot(dl,dl)<=rad*rad;
}
function tryHit(f,o){
  const m=f.mv;
  const er2=(m===M.K&&f.style==='lunge'&&(f.kn||0)===1)?.88:m.reach,lx=f.x+f.ax*er2,lz=f.z+f.az*er2,ly=f.y+m.h;   // DQ26a: posture-bound reach - BLUE's sweep foot truly reaches .88 from the DQ25 stance (its wide low arc cannot extend to M.K's 1.15), so its strike point is tested at .88; all other strikes keep m.reach
  if(XIAO_SWEEP&&m!==M.L&&!m.throw){if(!sweepHit(f,o))return} // XIAO: swept-tip trial path (P/K only; launcher/throw keep the original test)
  else{if(Math.hypot(lx-o.x,lz-o.z)>(m.throw?HURT_T:HURT))return;if(ly<o.y-.1||ly>o.y+1.8)return} // sidestepping pulls o out of the strike line
  if(o.st==='down'||o.st==='getup')return;   // DQ17i: the gradual get-up is uninterrupted, like down
  if(m.throw){
    if(o.y>.05||!['idle','walk','guard','step'].includes(o.st))return;
    f.hit=1;o.hp=Math.max(0,o.hp-m.dmg);o.carryFrom=pose(o);o.throwStyle=f.style;if(o.hp<=0){o.st='air';o.vy=4.8;o.t=0;o.ic='mid';o.rm=0;o.gy=-.08;o.kp=22;o.x=f.x+f.ax*.8;o.z=f.z+f.az*.8;o.vx=f.ax*m.kb;o.vz=f.az*m.kb;spark(o.x,1,o.z,1);hs=8;shake=.4;endRound(f,'K.O.');return}o.st='carry';o.t=0;o.vy=0;o.vx=0;o.vz=0;o.ic='mid';o.rm=0;o.gy=-.08;o.kp=22;
    o.x=f.x+f.ax*.8;o.z=f.z+f.az*.8;
    spark(o.x,1,o.z,1);hs=8;shake=.4;if(o.hp<=0)endRound(f,'K.O.');return;
  }
  f.hit=1;
  const air=o.y>.05;
  const facing=(o.fx*f.ax+o.fz*f.az)<-.3;
  if(o.st==='guard'&&facing&&!air){           // BLOCK: chip damage, blockstun, pushback
    const pwB=m===M.P?m.dmg+(f.pn||0)*2:(m===M.K?f.style==='lunge'?(f.kn||0)===1?12:(f.kn||0)===2?15:14:(f.kn||0)===1?16:(f.kn||0)===2?13:14:m.dmg),bsf=m===M.P?pwB/8:(m===M.K?pwB/14:1);o.hp=Math.max(1,o.hp-m.dmg*.1);o.st='stun';o.t=0;o.dur=Math.round(m.bs*bsf);o.imp=m.kb/5*bsf;   // DQ19 blockstun channel: blockstun duration and block recoil scale with per-strike power for punches (bsf=pw/8: jab/palm 7/.44 unchanged, cross/straight 9/.55, hook/chop 11/.66, uppercut/flow 12/.77, DQ18e slice 4: fifth strikes (overhand/back palm) pw 16 -> 14/.88); punch variants scale by pw/8; DQ19 kick-variant channel (cycle 46): kicks scale by pw/14 (BLUE snap pw 14 unchanged 12/.64, sweep pw 12 -> 10/.549; DQ18e: RED front pw 14 unchanged, roundhouse pw 16 -> 14/.731; DQ18e slice 2: BLUE side pw 15 -> 13/.686; DQ18e slice 3: RED crescent pw 13 -> 11/.594); launcher keeps class bs exactly; chip damage unchanged
    o.vx=f.ax*m.kb*.5*bsf;o.vz=f.az*m.kb*.5*bsf;f.vx-=f.ax*1.2;f.vz-=f.az*1.2;   // DQ19 blockstun channel: block pushback velocity scales with bsf (punches: jab 1.1 unchanged, cross 1.375, hook 1.65, uppercut 1.925; kicks: snap/front 1.6 unchanged, sweep 1.371, roundhouse 1.829, BLUE side 1.714, RED crescent 1.486)
    spark(lx,ly,lz,0);hs=3;return;
  }
  const sc=Math.max(.4,1-.15*o.combo);       // damage scaling in combos
  o.combo++;cmb=o.combo;cmbT=90;
  o.ic=(m===M.K&&f.style==='lunge'&&(f.kn||0)===1)?'low':(m.h>=1.2?'high':(m.h<=.95?'low':'mid'));   // DQ17c: impact class from strike height; DQ19 kick-variant channel: BLUE's sweep strikes at ankle height (pose foot y .05) so its impact class is low (face-down fall family) while the snap kick stays mid
  const pw=m===M.P?m.dmg+(f.pn||0)*2:(m===M.K?f.style==='lunge'?(f.kn||0)===1?12:(f.kn||0)===2?15:14:(f.kn||0)===1?16:(f.kn||0)===2?13:14:m.dmg);o.kp=pw;
  o.hp=Math.max(0,o.hp-pw*sc*(f.desp?.6:1));   // DQ19 damage channel: standing-hit damage = per-strike power pw (for non-punches pw===m.dmg, so kick 14 / launcher 16 are byte-identical in effect; jab/palm stay 8, cross/straight 10, hook/chop 12, uppercut/flow 14); DQ17j: a desperation strike from the stumble hits at x0.6 power   // DQ17k: per-strike power for the standing-hit stumble band - punches scale by style strike order (jab/palm 8, cross/straight 10, hook/chop 12, uppercut/flowing palm 14), kick 14, launcher 16
  if(m.launch||air){o.st='air';o.vy=m.launch||4.5;o.gy=o.ic==='low'?-.002:-.08}else if(pw>=12){o.st='stumble';const sev=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(pw-8)/8));o.sdur=Math.round(24+12*sev);o.ssteps=1+Math.round(2*sev);o.samp=.14+.26*sev;o.ssev=sev;o.sdesp=R()<.15?1:0;o.sdone=0;o.srem=0;o.desp=0}else{o.st='stun';o.dur=Math.round(m.hs*pw/8);o.imp=m.kb/3*(pw/8)}   // DQ19: hitstun duration and stun-pose recoil scale with per-strike power pw/8 - jab/palm (pw 8) unchanged at hs/imp baseline, cross/straight (pw 10) proportionally longer/stronger   // DQ17g: gy = -(LAND pose's lowest rendered joint: face-down knee 0.002 / back-down foot 0.080, measured on a92304d4); DQ17k: standing hits with pw>=12 stumble instead of stun (band between recoil and knockdown) - DQ17j COM-step/desperation machinery reused, severity scales with pw
  o.t=0;const kv=m.kb*(m===M.P?pw/8:(m===M.K?pw/14:1));o.vx=f.ax*kv*(f.desp?.6:1);o.vz=f.az*kv*(f.desp?.6:1);   // DQ19: knockback velocity scales with per-strike power for punches (pw/8: jab 2.2 unchanged, cross 2.75, hook 3.3, uppercut 3.85, DQ18e slice 4: fifth strikes 4.4); launcher keeps its class kb exactly; DQ19 kick-variant channel: kicks scale by pw/14 (snap/front 3.2 unchanged, sweep 2.743, DQ18e roundhouse 3.657, BLUE side 3.429, RED crescent 2.971)
  spark(lx,ly,lz,1);hs=6;shake=.25;
  if(o.hp<=0){o.st='air';o.vy=5;o.vx=f.ax*6;o.vz=f.az*6;endRound(f,'K.O.')}
}

function endRound(w,m){
  if(phase!=='fight')return;
  phase='end';pt=0;msg=m;msgT=200;if(w)w.wins++;
}

// ================= PER-FIGHTER UPDATE =================
function update(f,o){
  const i=f.i,dx=o.x-f.x,dz=o.z-f.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;
  f.t++;if(f.stunExit){if(f.st==='idle'){f.stunExitT++;if(f.stunExitT>=10)f.stunExit=null}else f.stunExit=null}if(f.walkExit){if(f.st==='idle'){f.walkExitT++;if(f.walkExitT>=10)f.walkExit=null}else f.walkExit=null}if(f.stepExit){if(f.st==='idle'){f.stepExitT++;if(f.stepExitT>=10)f.stepExit=null}else f.stepExit=null}if(f.throwExit){if(f.st==='idle'){f.throwExitT++;if(f.throwExitT>=10)f.throwExit=null}else f.throwExit=null}if(f.cd>0)f.cd--; // DQ93: step-exit timer — parallel to stunExit/walkExit; clears after 10 idle frames or at once on any other state.
  if(f.stepEnter){if(f.st==='step'){f.stepEnterT++;if(f.stepEnterT>=10)f.stepEnter=null}else f.stepEnter=null} // DQ94: step-enter timer — mirrors stepExit; clears after 10 step frames or at once on any other state.
  if(f.walkEnter){if(f.st==='walk'){f.walkEnterT++;if(f.walkEnterT>=10)f.walkEnter=null}else f.walkEnter=null} // DQ95: walk-enter timer — mirrors stepEnter; clears after 10 walk frames or at once on any other state.
  if(f.guardEnter){if(f.st==='guard'){f.guardEnterT++;if(f.guardEnterT>=10)f.guardEnter=null}else f.guardEnter=null} // DQ96: guard-enter timer — mirrors walkEnter; clears after 10 guard frames or at once on any other state.
  if(f.guardExit){if(f.st==='idle'){f.guardExitT++;if(f.guardExitT>=10)f.guardExit=null}else f.guardExit=null} // DQ96: guard-exit timer — mirrors stepExit; clears after 10 idle frames or at once on any other state.
  if(f.st!=='atk'&&f.buf)f.buf=null;   // DQ18d: an interrupted attack drops its buffered chain - no stale combos
  if(f.st!=='atk'&&f.desp)f.desp=0;   // DQ17j: an interrupted desperation strike loses its reduced-power flag
  if(f.combo>0&&o.st!=='atk'&&!['stun','air','down','getup','stumble'].includes(f.st))f.combo=0;   // DQ18d: the combo count survives hitstun only during a live chain - it clears once the attacker's attack ends
  const free=['idle','walk','guard','step'].includes(f.st);
  if(free&&f.y<=0){f.fx=dx/d;f.fz=dz/d}       // always face opponent (VF auto-turn)
  const rx=-f.fz,rz=f.fx;
  if(free&&phase==='fight'){
    let acted=false;
    if(i.bp>0&&i.bk>0)acted=go(f,'L');
    else if(i.g&&i.bp>0&&d<1.4)acted=go(f,'T');
    else if(i.bp>0)acted=go(f,'P');
    else if(i.bk>0)acted=go(f,'K');
    if(!acted){ // SUPPORT-FOOT GATE follow-through: a refused attack must not freeze the fighter — fall through to guard/movement so the walk continues and the held input fires when the foot plants.
    if(i.g&&f.st!=='guard'){f.guardEnter=pose(f);f.guardEnterT=0} // DQ96: capture the pre-guard pose (idle/walk/step) before the state flip so guard blends in instead of snapping.
    if(i.g){f.st='guard';f.vx*=.7;f.vz*=.7}
    else{
      const mf=i.r-i.l,ms=i.d-i.u;
      if(ms&&f.cd<=0&&f.st!=='step'){f.stepEnter=pose(f);f.stepEnterT=0;f.st='step';f.t=0;f.sd=ms}
      if(f.st==='step'){
        if(f.t>12){f.stepExit=pose(f);f.stepExitT=0;f.st='idle';f.cd=6}else{f.vx=rx*f.sd*STEPV;f.vz=rz*f.sd*STEPV} // DQ93: capture the last step pose BEFORE the state flips (pose(f) still sees 'step').
      }else{
        if(f.st==='guard'&&!mf){f.guardExit=pose(f);f.guardExitT=0} // DQ96: capture the guard pose before release; fires only for guard->idle (mf false), guard->walk is covered by the DQ95 walkEnter capture.
        if(f.st==='walk'&&!mf&&f.style==='lunge'){f.walkExit=pose(f);f.walkExitT=0}
        if(mf&&f.st!=='walk'&&f.style==='lunge'){f.walkEnter=pose(f);f.walkEnterT=0} // DQ95: capture the last idle/guard pose BEFORE the state flip (BLUE only) so the walk eases in.
        const sp=mf>0?FWD:BACK;f.vx=f.fx*mf*sp;f.vz=f.fz*mf*sp;f.st=mf?'walk':'idle';
        if(mf)f.w+=(mf*sp*DT)/CYCLE_DIST*2*Math.PI;   // R2: phase advances by signed distance travelled
      }
    }
    }
  }else if(free){f.vx*=.8;f.vz*=.8;f.st='idle'}
  if(f.st==='atk'){
    const m=f.mv;f.vx*=.85;f.vz*=.85;
    if(f.chainBlend&&f.t<=m.su+m.ac){f.vx=0;f.vz=0;f.x+=f.ax*CHAIN_DRIFT;f.z+=f.az*CHAIN_DRIFT} // C2: chained links drift forward (Kev 2026-10-27: forward drift) — root only; pose() compensates the feet so nothing slides. DQ89: chain drift now also applies to chained lunge (kick) links, so every link of a long chain closes the knockback gap; fresh kicks keep their lunge velocity (chainBlend is 0 fresh); larger drift values (0.045/0.040) were scratch-tested and REJECTED (overshoot, break KKKK/KPKPK/close-range chains).
    if(!f.desp&&f.t>=m.su&&(f.i.bp>0||f.i.bk>0)){f.buf=(f.i.bp>0&&f.i.bk>0)?'L':f.i.bp>0?'P':'K';f.i.bp=f.i.bk=0}   // DQ18d: chain window - an attack input from the first active frame on is buffered for a combo chain instead of expiring
    if(f.t>=m.su&&f.t<m.su+m.ac&&!f.hit)tryHit(f,o);
    const chainAt=m.su+m.ac+(m.rc>>1);   // DQ18d: a connected strike cancels into its buffered chain at half recovery - fluid, no idle frame between attacks; a whiffed strike's buffer fires only at the natural end of recovery
    if(f.buf&&f.hit&&f.t>=chainAt){const bk=f.buf;f.buf=null;go(f,bk)}
    else if(f.t>=m.su+m.ac+m.rc+((f.mv===M.T&&f.throwMove==='ippon')?8:0)){if(f.buf){const bk=f.buf;f.buf=null;go(f,bk)}else if(f.desp){f.desp=0;f.st='stumble';f.t=Math.max(0,f.sdur-f.srem)}else{if(f.mv===M.T){f.throwExit=pose(f);f.throwExitT=0}f.st='idle'}}   // DQ17j: a desperation strike returns to the stumble, extended by the balance it cost
  }else if(f.st==='stun'){f.vx*=.88;f.vz*=.88;if(f.t>=f.dur){f.stunExit=pose(f);f.stunExitT=0;f.st='idle';if(o.st!=='atk')f.combo=0}}   // DQ18d: hitstun ending mid-chain does not reset the combo count while the attacker is still attacking
  else if(f.st==='down'){f.vx*=.9;f.vz*=.9;if(f.t>=55&&f.hp>0){f.st='getup';f.t=0;f.combo=0;f.gy=0}}   // DQ17i: down now hands off to the gradual get-up instead of snapping to idle
  else if(f.st==='getup'){f.vx*=.9;f.vz*=.9;if(f.t>=54&&f.hp>0){f.st='idle'}}   // DQ17i: 54f staged rise (3x 18f phases in pose()); DQ17k: the rise returns cleanly to stance - no stumble after a fall, ever (Kev correction 2026-10-26 ~11:33: stumbling is a standing hit reaction, not a post-fall behavior)
  else if(f.st==='stumble'){   // DQ17j: standing recovery - corrective steps chase the drifting COM in the knockback direction (-facing); no guard/walk (not a free state); a ~15% desperation strike fires once, mid-stumble
    const ss=stubStep(f);
    if(ss.i>=0){const disp=(f.samp*.9)/ss.n,vv=disp/((f.sdur*(.44/ss.n))*DT)*(Math.PI/2)*Math.sin(Math.PI*ss.p);f.vx=-f.fx*vv;f.vz=-f.fz*vv}else{f.vx*=.8;f.vz*=.8}
    if(f.sdesp&&!f.sdone&&f.t>=(f.sdur>>1)){f.sdone=1;f.srem=f.sdur-f.t+8;go(f,'P');f.desp=1}
    else if(f.t>=f.sdur){f.st='idle'}
  }
  if(f.st==='carry'){const THc=THROW_PHASES[f.throwStyle],CP=THc?throwPhase(THc,'carry'):null,LN=THc?throwPhase(THc,'launch'):null,uu=Math.min(1,f.t/(CP?CP.liftFrames:10)),lift=(f.throwStyle==='ippon'?(()=>{const uL=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(f.t-19)/5));return .65*uL*uL*(3-2*uL)})():(CP?CP.lift:.60)*uu*uu*(3-2*uu));const anch=(THc===THROW_PHASES.shoulder)?0.8-0.30*(uu*uu*(3-2*uu)):(THc===THROW_PHASES.ippon)?0.8-0.50*(uu*uu*(3-2*uu)):0.8;f.x=o.x+o.ax*anch;f.z=o.z+o.az*.8;f.y=lift;f.vx=0;f.vz=0;f.vy=0;if(f.t>=(CP?CP.release:12)){if(LN&&LN.ic)f.ic=LN.ic;f.airFrom=pose(f);f.st='air';f.t=0;f.vy=LN?LN.vy:4.8;f.vx=o.ax*(LN?LN.vx:3);f.vz=o.az*(LN?LN.vx:3);f.carryFrom=null;f.throwStyle=null}return}
  // ---- physics: semi-implicit Euler, gravity, ring-only ground ----
  f.vy-=GRAV*DT*(f.st==='air'?1.5:1);   // DQ17c: heavier descent while airborne
  f.x+=f.vx*DT;f.z+=f.vz*DT;f.y+=f.vy*DT;
  const on=Math.abs(f.x)<RING&&Math.abs(f.z)<RING;
  if(on&&f.y<=0){
    if(f.st==='air'){
      const hm=Math.hypot(f.vx,f.vz),ds=-f.vy;f.rm=hm;   // DQ17e: touchdown momentum (horizontal speed, descent speed); DQ17f: rm records it for the roll-through check in pose()
      f.st='down';f.t=0;f.gy=f.ic==='low'?-.002:-.08;const sf=.5+.35*Math.min(1,hm/5);f.vx*=sf;f.vz*=sf;   // DQ17e: ground slide - retained horizontal fraction scales with touchdown momentum (was flat .5)
      f.vy=(ds>=5&&hm>=2)?Math.min(1.5,ds*.24):0;   // DQ17e: one small bounce off a fast touchdown - down-state rebound, pose stays on the touchdown blend
      shake=Math.max(shake,.2);spark(f.x,.05,f.z,0)

    f.y=0;
  }else if(!on&&f.y<-.05&&f.st!=='air'){f.st='air'}
  if(!on&&f.y<-2.5)endRound(o,'RING OUT!');
  if(phase!=='fight'&&f.y<-3){f.y=-3;f.vy=0;if(f.st==='air')f.st='down'}  // park fallen fighters after the round (default-build fix 2026-10-20)
}

// ================= AI =================
function ai(f,o){
  const i=f.i;if(f.aiT-->0)return;f.aiT=8+(R()*12|0);
  i.l=i.r=i.u=i.d=i.g=0;
  const d=Math.hypot(o.x-f.x,o.z-f.z),threat=o.st==='atk';
  if(threat&&d<1.6&&R()<.5){i.g=1;f.aiT=14;return}
  if(threat&&d<1.6&&R()<.3){i[R()<.5?'u':'d']=1;f.aiT=10;return}
  if(d>1.1){i.r=1;if(R()<.15)i[R()<.5?'u':'d']=1}
  else{const r=R();
    if(r<.3)i.bp=6;else if(r<.5)i.bk=6;else if(r<.58){i.bp=6;i.bk=6}
    else if(r<.66){i.g=1;i.bp=6}else if(r<.8)i.g=1;else i.l=1}
}

// ================= MAIN TICK =================
function tick(){
  if(hs>0){hs--;return}
  const[a,b]=S;
  Object.assign(a.i,H0);
  if(phase==='intro'){if(++pt>90){phase='fight';msg='FIGHT!';msgT=50}}
  if(phase==='fight'){ai(b,a);if(--tm<=0){tm=0;
    endRound(a.hp>b.hp?a:b.hp>a.hp?b:null,a.hp===b.hp?'DRAW':'TIME UP')}}
  for(const f of S){if(f.i.bp>0)f.i.bp--;if(f.i.bk>0)f.i.bk--}
  update(a,b);update(b,a);
  const d=Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.z-a.z);               // body-body separation
  if(d<.6&&a.y<.6&&b.y<.6){const p=(.6-d)/2,nx=(b.x-a.x)/(d||1),nz=(b.z-a.z)/(d||1);a.x-=nx*p;a.z-=nz*p;b.x+=nx*p;b.z+=nz*p}
  // camera follows the line between fighters
  const ty=Math.atan2(b.z-a.z,b.x-a.x);let dy=ty-cam.yaw;dy=Math.atan2(Math.sin(dy),Math.cos(dy));
  cam.yaw+=dy*.12;cam.mx+=((a.x+b.x)/2-cam.mx)*.15;cam.mz+=((a.z+b.z)/2-cam.mz)*.15;
  cam.D+=(11+d*.5-cam.D)*.1;
  shake*=.88;if(cmbT>0)cmbT--;if(msgT>0)msgT--;
  for(const s of sparks)s.t++;sparks=sparks.filter(s=>s.t<14);
  if(phase==='end'&&++pt>160){
    if(S[0].wins>=2||S[1].wins>=2){phase='over';msg=(S[0].wins>=2?'YOU WIN':'YOU LOSE')+' – TAP TO REMATCH';msgT=1e9}
    else{round++;reset(0)}
  }
}

// ================= RENDER =================
let ox=0,oy=0;
function P(x,y,z){
  const F=Math.min(W*1.5,H*1.55),rx=x-cam.mx,rz=z-cam.mz,c=Math.cos(cam.yaw),s=Math.sin(cam.yaw);
  const a=rx*c+rz*s,dp=-rx*s+rz*c,k=F/Math.max(2,cam.D+dp);
  return{x:W/2+a*k+ox,y:H*.4+(2.4-y)*k+oy,s:k,d:dp};
}
// ---- R1 JOINT LAYER (rig track step R1): analytic two-bone IK under pose() ----
// Segment constants (m): arm 0.40+0.40 (reach 0.80), leg 0.62+0.62 (reach 1.24).
// Elbows/knees derive from root anchor + endpoint + lengths only; hands/feet never move.
// R2: single continuous bend = cross(u,[0,0,1]); sg=-1 knees (bow +x/+y), sg=+1 elbows (bow -x/-y). No pole blend.
const ARM1=.40,ARM2=.40,LEG1=.62,LEG2=.62;
function ik2(r,e,L1,L2,sg){
  const dx=e[0]-r[0],dy=e[1]-r[1],dz=e[2]-r[2],rd=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);
  const u=rd>1e-6?[dx/rd,dy/rd,dz/rd]:[1,0,0];
  const d=Math.min(Math.max(rd,.05),L1+L2-.005);      // clamp solve distance only; endpoint untouched
  const bn=Math.hypot(u[1],u[0]);                      // |cross(u,z)|; bendRaw=[u1,-u0,0] is exactly perpendicular to u
  let v;
  if(bn>=1e-4)v=[sg*u[1]/bn,-sg*u[0]/bn,0];
  else{const t=-sg*u[0];v=[-sg-t*u[0],-t*u[1],-t*u[2]]}   // degenerate (u ~ +-z): old primary-pole perpendicular
  const vl=Math.hypot(v[0],v[1],v[2])||1;              // ~1 on the main path; keeps fallback finite
  const a=(L1*L1-L2*L2+d*d)/(2*d),h=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,L1*L1-a*a));
  return[r[0]+u[0]*a+v[0]/vl*h,r[1]+u[1]*a+v[1]/vl*h,r[2]+u[2]*a+v[2]/vl*h];
}
// R2: phase-locked linear stance foot. c = cycle fraction (any real, normalised here). Returns [dx,y] relative to base x.
// Stance (c<.5): x sweeps +STEP/2 -> -STEP/2 linearly, y=0. Swing: x returns linearly, y = .12*sin(pi*swingFrac).
function ik2Pole(r,e,L1,L2,prev,fallbackSg){
  const dx=e[0]-r[0],dy=e[1]-r[1],dz=e[2]-r[2],rd=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);
  const u=rd>1e-6?[dx/rd,dy/rd,dz/rd]:[1,0,0];
  const d=Math.min(Math.max(rd,.05),L1+L2-.005);
  const a=(L1*L1-L2*L2+d*d)/(2*d),h=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,L1*L1-a*a));
  let w=[prev[0]-r[0],prev[1]-r[1],prev[2]-r[2]];
  const k=w[0]*u[0]+w[1]*u[1]+w[2]*u[2];
  let v=[w[0]-k*u[0],w[1]-k*u[1],w[2]-k*u[2]];
  let vl=Math.hypot(v[0],v[1],v[2]);
  if(vl<1e-4){return ik2(r,e,L1,L2,fallbackSg);}
  v=[v[0]/vl,v[1]/vl,v[2]/vl];

function ikFlat(r,e,sd){  // DQ17h: ground-plane arm IK - same a/h construction as ik2, but the bend direction is the component of [0,0,sd] perpendicular to the shoulder->hand axis (elbows splay flat/outward instead of bowing down)
  const dx=e[0]-r[0],dy=e[1]-r[1],dz=e[2]-r[2],rd=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);
  const u=rd>1e-6?[dx/rd,dy/rd,dz/rd]:[1,0,0];
  const d=Math.min(Math.max(rd,.05),ARM1+ARM2-.005);
  const wu=sd*u[2];let v=[-wu*u[0],-wu*u[1],sd-wu*u[2]];
  const vl=Math.hypot(v[0],v[1],v[2]);
  v=vl<1e-4?[0,1,0]:[v[0]/vl,v[1]/vl,v[2]/vl];
  const a=(ARM1*ARM1-ARM2*ARM2+d*d)/(2*d),h=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,ARM1*ARM1-a*a));
  return[r[0]+u[0]*a+v[0]*h,r[1]+u[1]*a+v[1]*h,r[2]+u[2]*a+v[2]*h];
}
function ikBend(r,e,sd,fw,pole,st,tk){  // DQ17i: arm IK with the bend direction blended between ik2's standard bend (fw=0) and ikFlat's ground-plane bend (fw=1); same a/h construction as both, so arm segment lengths stay 0.40+0.40 at every blend
  // MERGE 159-166: 161 slew state added (st={tk,dir}, tk="state:tick"); caps bend-direction rotation at 0.35 rad/tick; ticks under the cap are byte-identical to the uncapped solution.
  // CYCLE160: pole = the standard-bend pole the elbow must land on when fw reaches 0 (defaults to sd). Previously vs was always the +1 pole, so a lunge rear elbow (final pole -1) flipped at the getup fw=0 boundary (tick 35->36, 0.702 m). Passing the style's final pole makes ikBend(0) exactly equal the post-blend ik2 pole, so the boundary is continuous. Down (fw=1) and getup phase 1 (fw=1) are untouched: vs has zero weight there.
  pole=pole===undefined?sd:pole;
  const dx=e[0]-r[0],dy=e[1]-r[1],dz=e[2]-r[2],rd=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);
  const u=rd>1e-6?[dx/rd,dy/rd,dz/rd]:[1,0,0];
  const d=Math.min(Math.max(rd,.05),ARM1+ARM2-.005);
  const bn=Math.hypot(u[1],u[0]);let vs;
  if(bn>=1e-4)vs=[pole*u[1]/bn,-pole*u[0]/bn,0];
  else{const t=-pole*u[0];vs=[-pole-t*u[0],-t*u[1],-t*u[2]];const l=Math.hypot(vs[0],vs[1],vs[2])||1;vs=[vs[0]/l,vs[1]/l,vs[2]/l]}
  const wu=sd*u[2];let vf=[-wu*u[0],-wu*u[1],sd-wu*u[2]];
  const vl=Math.hypot(vf[0],vf[1],vf[2]);vf=vl<1e-4?[0,1,0]:[vf[0]/vl,vf[1]/vl,vf[2]/vl];
  let v=[vs[0]*(1-fw)+vf[0]*fw,vs[1]*(1-fw)+vf[1]*fw,vs[2]*(1-fw)+vf[2]*fw];
  const wl=Math.hypot(v[0],v[1],v[2]);v=wl<1e-4?vs:[v[0]/wl,v[1]/wl,v[2]/wl];
  if(st){if(st.tk===tk&&st.dir){v=st.dir.slice()}else{if(st.dir){let dt=v[0]*st.dir[0]+v[1]*st.dir[1]+v[2]*st.dir[2];dt=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,dt));const ang=Math.acos(dt);if(ang>0.35){const sn=Math.sin(ang);v=[(st.dir[0]*Math.sin(ang-0.35)+v[0]*Math.sin(0.35))/sn,(st.dir[1]*Math.sin(ang-0.35)+v[1]*Math.sin(0.35))/sn,(st.dir[2]*Math.sin(ang-0.35)+v[2]*Math.sin(0.35))/sn]}}st.tk=tk;st.dir=v.slice()}}
  const a=(ARM1*ARM1-ARM2*ARM2+d*d)/(2*d),h=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,ARM1*ARM1-a*a));
  return[r[0]+u[0]*a+v[0]*h,r[1]+u[1]*a+v[1]*h,r[2]+u[2]*a+v[2]*h];
}
function gaitFoot(c){
  c-=Math.floor(c);
  if(c<.5)return[STEP_LEN*(.5-c*2),0];
  const q=(c-.5)*2;
  return[STEP_LEN*(q-.5),Math.sin(q*Math.PI)*.14];
}
function stubStep(f){   // DQ17j: corrective-step phase for the stumble - steps spread over the first 80% of the window, each step window 55% of its segment; returns {u, i (active step index or -1), p (phase 0..1), n}
  const dur=f.sdur||30,u=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,f.t/dur)),n=f.ssteps||1,tw=.8/n*.55;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const c=(i+.5)/n*.8;if(Math.abs(u-c)<=tw/2)return{u,i,p:(u-(c-tw/2))/tw,n}}
  return{u,i:-1,p:0,n};
}

j=1;j<7;j++){const   if(s==='air'&&f.ic==='ippon'&&f.airFrom&&(f.t||0)<=6){const _bU=Math.min(1,(f.t||0)/6),_bW=_bU*_bU*(3-2*_bU),_bF=f.airFrom;for(const _j of['el','er','kl','kr']){const _q=p[_j],_r=_bF[_j];if(_r&&_q)p[_j]=[_r[0]+(_q[0]-_r[0])*_bW,_r[1]+(_q[1]-_r[1])*_bW,_r[2]+(_q[2]-_r[2])*_bW];}} // CYCLE154b: blend elbows/knees from carry-end pose too - carry elbows/knees are carryFrom-blended, not pure IK, so endpoint blending alone left 0.23-0.43 m elbow/knee jumps at release; post-IK blend closes them, segment lengths re-checked by the contact probe
p.rotS=rotS;p.rotH=rotH;
  if(s==='air'){f._airEl=p.el.slice();f._airEr=p.er.slice();} // CYCLE159 PHASE 1: remember last air elbows for touchdown continuity
  return p;
}
function ln(a,b,w,c){g.strokeStyle=c;g.lineWidth=w;g.lineCap='round';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a.x,a.y);g.lineTo(b.x,b.y);g.stroke()}
// R4 STICK RETURN (Kev direct 2026-10-21): clean stick figures - uniform limbs, solid silhouette colour, no taper, no gloss. Rig/IK underneath unchanged.
const LEGW=.15,ARMW=.13;
function jt(p,r,col){g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.arc(p.x,p.y,r,0,7);g.fill()}
function limb(r,m,e,w,col,k){
  ln(r,m,w*k,col);ln(m,e,w*k,col);
  jt(r,w*k/2,col);jt(m,w*k/2,col);jt(e,w*k/2,col);
}
function segOne(a,b,w,col,k){   // one independent segment drawable: d = avg projected depth of its 2 endpoints
  return{d:(a.d+b.d)/2,draw(){ln(a,b,w*k,col);jt(a,w*k/2,col);jt(b,w*k/2,col)}};
}
function segLeg(a,m,b,w,col,k){   // DQ29 BLENDED LEG (Kev 2026-10-27): one continuous curve through the knee - quadratic through the knee point to the knee->foot midpoint, then straight to the foot (tangents match at the join, so the corner rounds); NO joint circle at the knee, so the leg reads as one blended limb, not two segments hinging at a prominent knee. Hip/foot joint circles only.
  return{d:(a.d+m.d+b.d)/3,draw(){g.strokeStyle=col;g.lineWidth=w*k;g.lineCap='round';g.lineJoin='round';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a.x,a.y);g.quadraticCurveTo(m.x,m.y,(m.x+b.x)/2,(m.y+b.y)/2);g.lineTo(b.x,b.y);g.stroke();jt(a,w*k/2,col);jt(b,w*k/2,col)}};
}
function drawF(f,col){
  const p=pose(f),rx=-f.fz,rz=f.fx;
  const J=q=>P(f.x+f.fx*q[0]+rx*q[2],f.y+q[1],f.z+f.fz*q[0]+rz*q[2]);
  const sh=P(f.x,0,f.z);
  if(Math.abs(f.x)<RING&&Math.abs(f.z)<RING){g.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.25)';g.beginPath();g.ellipse(sh.x,sh.y,.42*sh.s,.14*sh.s,0,0,7);g.fill()}
  const A=J(p.pel),N=J(p.neck),Hd=J(p.head),k=A.s;
  const sl=J([p.neck[0]+.11*(p.rotS||0),p.neck[1]-.05,.22-.03*(p.rotS||0)]),sr=J([p.neck[0]-.11*(p.rotS||0),p.neck[1]-.05,-.22+.03*(p.rotS||0)]);
  const hl=J([p.pel[0]+.04*(p.rotH||0),p.pel[1],p.pel[2]+.12-.009*(p.rotH||0)]),hr=J([p.pel[0]-.04*(p.rotH||0),p.pel[1],p.pel[2]-.12+.009*(p.rotH||0)]);
  const atkOn=f.st==='atk'&&f.t>=f.mv.su&&f.t<f.mv.su+f.mv.ac;
  const el=J(p.el),hL=J(p.hl),kl=J(p.kl),fl=J(p.fl),er=J(p.er),hR=J(p.hr),kr=J(p.kr),fr=J(p.fr);
  const aLc=atkOn?'#fff':col,lRc=atkOn?'#fff':col;
  const limbs=[
    segOne(sl,el,ARMW,aLc,k),segOne(el,hL,ARMW,aLc,k),     // armL (punch highlight, as before)
    segOne(sr,er,ARMW,col,k),segOne(er,hR,ARMW,col,k),     // armR
    segLeg(hl,kl,fl,LEGW,col,k),      // legL (DQ29 blended curve through the knee)
    segLeg(hr,kr,fr,LEGW,lRc,k)       // legR (kick highlight, as before; DQ29 blended)
  ];
  const td=(A.d+N.d)/2;   // torso depth; larger projected d = farther = drawn first
  const byFar=(a,b)=>b.d-a.d;
  const behind=limbs.filter(l=>l.d>=td).sort(byFar),front=limbs.filter(l=>l.d<td).sort(byFar);
  behind.forEach(l=>l.draw());
  ln(A,N,.17*k,col);
  g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.arc(Hd.x,Hd.y,.17*k,0,7);g.fill();   // faceless head
  front.forEach(l=>l.draw());
}
function draw(){
  ox=(R()-.5)*shake*30;oy=(R()-.5)*shake*30;
  const bg=css('--bg'),fg=css('--fg'),ring=css('--ring'),ac=css('--accent'),bl=css('--blue');
  g.fillStyle=bg;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  // R4 BACKDROP (Kev direct 2026-10-21): procedural Fuji scene per canonical staff-duel reference - golden-hour sky, snow-capped Fuji, blossom clusters, wet reflective ground.
  const hz=H*.46;
  const sky=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,hz*1.15);
  sky.addColorStop(0,'#f6d8a8');sky.addColorStop(.45,'#e8a87c');sky.addColorStop(.75,'#9db4c8');sky.addColorStop(1,'#6e8299');
  g.fillStyle=sky;g.fillRect(0,0,W,hz+2);
  const sun=g.createRadialGradient(W*.32,H*.16,4,W*.32,H*.16,W*.28);
  sun.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,236,190,.95)');sun.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,236,190,0)');
  g.fillStyle=sun;g.fillRect(0,0,W,hz+2);
  const fx=W*.58,fw=W*.30,fa=H*.13,fb=hz+4;
  g.fillStyle='#66788f';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(fx-fw,fb);g.lineTo(fx,fa);g.lineTo(fx+fw,fb);g.closePath();g.fill();
  g.fillStyle='#f4f7f9';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(fx-fw*.22,fa+(fb-fa)*.22);g.lineTo(fx,fa);g.lineTo(fx+fw*.22,fa+(fb-fa)*.22);g.lineTo(fx+fw*.07,fa+(fb-fa)*.32);g.lineTo(fx,fa+(fb-fa)*.26);g.lineTo(fx-fw*.07,fa+(fb-fa)*.32);g.closePath();g.fill();
  g.fillStyle='rgba(84,96,84,.55)';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,hz);for(let x=0;x<=W;x+=W/8)g.lineTo(x,hz-10-Math.sin(x*.01)*8);g.lineTo(W,hz+2);g.lineTo(0,hz+2);g.closePath();g.fill();
  g.fillStyle='rgba(246,194,202,.9)';
  for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const bx=i<7?W*.04+i*W*.03:W*.72+(i-7)*W*.035,by=hz-14-((i*37)%18),br=7+((i*29)%9);g.beginPath();g.arc(bx,by,br,0,7);g.fill()}
  const wet=g.createLinearGradient(0,hz,0,H);
  wet.addColorStop(0,'#9a8f7a');wet.addColorStop(.35,'#6f6a5e');wet.addColorStop(1,'#3d3a36');
  g.fillStyle=wet;g.fillRect(0,hz,W,H-hz);
  const cs=[[-RING,-RING],[RING,-RING],[RING,RING],[-RING,RING]].map(c=>P(c[0],0,c[1]));
  g.fillStyle=ring;g.beginPath();cs.forEach((c,i)=>i?g.lineTo(c.x,c.y):g.moveTo(c.x,c.y));g.closePath();g.fill();
  g.fillStyle='rgba(255,224,170,.16)';for(let i=0;i<4;i++)g.fillRect(W*.22+i*8,H*.60+i*H*.065,W*.42,3);   // wet-ground sun sheen streaks
  g.strokeStyle=fg;g.globalAlpha=.12;g.lineWidth=1;
  for(let k=-RING;k<=RING;k++){let a=P(k,0,-RING),b=P(k,0,RING);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a.x,a.y);g.lineTo(b.x,b.y);g.stroke();a=P(-RING,0,k);b=P(RING,0,k);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a.x,a.y);g.lineTo(b.x,b.y);g.stroke()}
  g.globalAlpha=.5;g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();cs.forEach((c,i)=>i?g.lineTo(c.x,c.y):g.moveTo(c.x,c.y));g.closePath();g.stroke();g.globalAlpha=1;
  const order=[[S[0],ac],[S[1],bl]].sort((a,b)=>P(b[0].x,0,b[0].z).d-P(a[0].x,0,a[0].z).d);
  for(const[f,c]of order)drawF(f,c);
  for(const s of sparks){const q=P(s.x,s.y,s.z),r=(s.t+2)*.012*q.s*3;g.strokeStyle=s.c?'#ffb020':bl;g.globalAlpha=1-s.t/14;g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.arc(q.x,q.y,r,0,7);g.stroke();g.globalAlpha=1}
  // HUD
  const bw=W*.4,bh=12,y=12;
  for(let n=0;n<2;n++){const f=S[n],x=n?W-W*.04-bw:W*.04;
    g.fillStyle=ring;g.fillRect(x,y,bw,bh);g.fillStyle=n?bl:ac;
    const w=bw*f.hp/100;g.fillRect(n?x+bw-w:x,y,w,bh);
    for(let k=0;k<2;k++){g.fillStyle=k<f.wins?(n?bl:ac):ring;g.beginPath();g.arc(n?x+bw-6-k*16:x+6+k*16,y+24,5,0,7);g.fill()}}
  g.fillStyle=fg;g.font='700 20px system-ui';g.textAlign='center';g.fillText(Math.ceil(tm/60),W/2,y+14);
  if(cmbT>0&&cmb>1){g.font='700 22px system-ui';g.fillStyle=ac;g.fillText(cmb+' HIT',W/2,y+58)}
  if(msgT>0){g.fillStyle=fg;g.font='800 '+Math.min(40,W/9)+'px system-ui';g.fillText(msg,W/2,H*.3)}
  if(phase==='intro'){g.font='13px system-ui';g.fillStyle=fg;g.globalAlpha=.7;g.fillText('◀▶ move · ▲▼ sidestep · P K · G guard · G+P throw · P+K launcher',W/2,H*.3+26);g.globalAlpha=1}
}

// ================= INPUT =================
function press(k,v){
  const i=S[0].i;
  if(k==='p'){if(v)i.bp=6}else if(k==='k'){if(v)i.bk=6}else if(k==='pk'){if(v){i.bp=6;i.bk=6}}
  else H0[k]=v?1:0;
}
document.querySelectorAll('button[data-k]').forEach(b=>{
  const k=b.dataset.k;
  b.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();b.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);b.classList.add('on');press(k,1)});
  ['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'].forEach(ev=>b.addEventListener(ev,()=>{b.classList.remove('on');press(k,0)}));
});
const KM={KeyA:'l',ArrowLeft:'l',KeyD:'r',ArrowRight:'r',KeyW:'u',ArrowUp:'u',KeyS:'d',ArrowDown:'d',KeyJ:'p',KeyK:'k',KeyL:'g',Space:'pk'};
addEventListener('keydown',e=>{const k=KM[e.code];if(k&&!e.repeat){e.preventDefault();press(k,1)}});
addEventListener('keyup',e=>{const k=KM[e.code];if(k)press(k,0)});
cv.addEventListener('pointerdown',()=>{if(phase==='over')reset(1)});

// ================= LOOP =================
let acc=0,last=performance.now();
function loop(now){acc+=Math.min(.1,(now-last)/1000);last=now;while(acc>=DT){tick();acc-=DT}draw();requestAnimationFrame(loop)}
requestAnimationFrame(loop);
</script></body></html>
```
